Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the number_theory space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 914 and the convergence rate is O(676/n^1).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on number_theory structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 914. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 676. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any number_theory module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 349. The projective resolution has length at most 357 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the number_theory decomposition. By minimality, the element a^349 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 357-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
